# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 1,
  "instanceId": "example",
  "role": "example"
}
```


## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, or one emoji/icon grapheme, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"AB"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"homeUnavailable":{"type":"string","enum":["home_missing","home_unreadable","foreign_home","home_conflict"]}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json

  "auth": "unknown",

    "source": "sdk-store",
    "status": "logged-in"
  },
  "observedAt": 1,
  "usage": {},
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cliHome | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeUsesInitial | no | boolean |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "authMethod": "unknown",
  "availability": "near_limit",
  "badgeUsesInitial": false,
  "cliHome": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "implicit": false,
  "isDefault": false,
  "label": "example",
  "profileRevision": "example",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_out",
    "billingMode": "unknown",
    "observedAt": 4,
    "usage": {
      "inputTokens": 5,
      "outputTokens": 1
    },
    "windows": {}
  },
  "shortLabel": "qZ[",
  "signedInAs": "example"
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json

  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": false,
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": true
  },
  "coverage": "source_profile",
  "mcp": "http",
  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": true
  },
  "subagentSessions": false,
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

## Agent

[JSON Schema](schema/Agent.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| childThreadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentId | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| origin | yes | [AgentOrigin.json](schema/AgentOrigin.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| native | yes | [NativeRef.json](schema/NativeRef.json) |  |
| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |
| background | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "background": true,
  "childThreadId": "example",
  "createdAt": 6,
  "cwd": "example",
  "endedAt": 2,
  "fidelity": "placeholder",
  "id": "example",
  "lineage": {
    "lossy": false,
    "mode": "portable",
```

### Variant 31

Semantic rule: Exactly one of url or number is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.link_pr"` |  |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| repo | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "thread.link_pr",
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

### Variant 32

Semantic rule: Exactly one of url, number, or all: true is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.unlink_pr"` |  |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| all | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "thread.unlink_pr",
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

## BrowserOrigin

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOrigin.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An exact canonical HTTP(S) origin, including scheme and optional port, with no path, query, fragment or URL credentials.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"https://youtube.com"
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

```json
  "throughSeq": 2,
  "type": "events"
}
```

## DaemonHealth

[JSON Schema](schema/DaemonHealth.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| running | yes | `true` |  |
| version | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"development"},{"type":"string","maxLength":80,"pattern":"^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+(?:-[a-z0-9.]+)?$","x-ace-constraint":"Daemon version must have a nonzero major version; legacy ace 0.x is incompatible."}]} |
| ready | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "ready": true,
  "running": true,
  "version": "development"
}
```

## DelegationRecord

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationRecord.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| parentAgentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |

| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| request | yes | [DelegationRequest.json](schema/DelegationRequest.json) |  |
| depth | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| phase | yes | ["created","running","cancelling","settled"] |  |
| generation | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |
| resolvedModel | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| resultDelivery | no | ["parent","owner"] |  |

```json
  "parentId": "example",

  "request": {

    "installationId": "example",

    "requestId": "KpKeq",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example"
  },

  "resolvedModel": "example",
  "rootId": "example"
}
```

| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "metrics": {
    "captureOverheadPct": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 8
    },
    "frames": {
      "median": 7,
      "worst": 6
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 8
    },
    "hitchRatioMsPerS": {
      "median": 7,
      "worst": 4
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 6,
      "worst": 8
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 7
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "high",
      "verdict": "inconclusive"
    }
  ]
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

```json
}
```

| url | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| state | yes | ["open","draft","merged","closed"] |  |
| title | no | string | {"maxLength":65536} |
| updatedAt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| unverified | no | boolean |  |
| deleted | no | boolean |  |

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |

```json
  "omittedItems": 4,
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectIcon.json), input validation.

| required | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

```json

  "default": false,
  "required": false,
  "type": "boolean"
}
```

| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}},"required":["name","sourceUrl"]} |
| commands | yes | array | {"maxItems":4,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InstallCommand.json"}} |
| verify | no | [InstallCommand.json](schema/InstallCommand.json) |  |
| sourceUrl | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| needsAdmin | yes | boolean |  |
| versionCheckedAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

| updateAvailable | no | boolean |  |

## ProviderInstallProgress

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstallProgress.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| session | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| agent | no | [InstallAgent.json](schema/InstallAgent.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^official:[a-z0-9-]+$"} |
| action | yes | [InstallAction.json](schema/InstallAction.json) |  |
| method | yes | [InstallMethod.json](schema/InstallMethod.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["planning","running","verifying","succeeded","failed","cancelled","needs_admin"] |  |
| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| step | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":4} |
| plan | no | [ProviderInstallPlan.json](schema/ProviderInstallPlan.json) |  |
| lines | yes | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048}} |
| exit | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":-9007199254740991,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| version | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| message | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "uninstall",
  "lines": [],
  "message": "example",
  "method": "registry",
  "plan": {
    "action": "install",
    "agent": "goose",
    "commands": [],
    "methods": [],
    "needsAdmin": false,
    "provider": "acp",
    "sourceUrl": "https://example.invalid/",
    "status": "ready"
  },
  "provider": "pi",
  "sequence": 1,
  "session": "example",
  "state": "planning",
  "step": 4,
  "version": "example"
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

## RemoteTaskPhase

[JSON Schema](schema/RemoteTaskPhase.json), input validation.

Type: ["queued","running","waiting","unavailable","cancelling","completed","failed","cancelled"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"failed"
```

| phase | yes | ["running","waiting","unavailable","cancelling","completed","failed","cancelled"] |  |

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |
| fingerprint | yes | [ReviewFingerprint.json](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","outdated","addressed-pending-review"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ]
}
```

```json
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| id | yes | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| trigger | yes | [RunTrigger.json](schema/RunTrigger.json) |  |
| nativeId | no | string |  |
| ordinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| checkpoints | no | [RunCheckpoints.json](schema/RunCheckpoints.json) |  |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","completed","interrupted","failed"] |  |
| startedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| error | no | [AgentError.json](schema/AgentError.json) |  |

```json
  "agentId": "example",

  "trigger": "unknown"
}
```

## ScreenAction

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenAction.json), input validation.

### click

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"click"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| button | no | ["left","right"] | {"default":"left"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "click",
  "x": 6,
  "y": 8
}
```

### type

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"type"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "type",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| keyCode | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":127} |
| modifiers | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":4,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["command","shift","option","control"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "keyCode": 2,
  "kind": "key",
  "modifiers": []
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| deltaX | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |
| deltaY | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 3,
  "deltaY": 8,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 2,
  "y": 8
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| background | no | boolean |  |
| windowSelection | no | boolean |  |
| permissionEvents | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "background": true,
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": false,
    "displays": false,
    "windows": false
  },
  "codecs": [
    "jpeg"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": true,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": true
  },
  "permissionEvents": true,
  "permissions": {
    "input": "n/a",
    "screen": "n/a"
  },
  "platform": "macos",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": false,
  "version": 2
}
```

| value | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},{"type":"boolean"},{"type":"object","properties":{"dx":{"default":0,"type":"integer","minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000},"dy":{"default":0,"type":"integer","minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000}}}]} |
| op | yes | `"ui.act"` |  |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |

Example:

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":9,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 9,
  "kind": "tool_call",
  "kinds": [
    "message"
  ],
  "status": "unresponsive",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

| providers.planner.tier | yes | ["default","standard","fast","flex","priority"] |  |
| providers.planner.reasoningEffort | yes | ["default","none","minimal","low","medium","high","xhigh","max"] |  |
| browser.allowedOrigins | yes | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserOrigin.json"}} |
| browser.backend | yes | ["auto","embedded","headless"] |  |
| browser.backendLoss | yes | ["pause","headless"] |  |
| browser.profile | yes | ["persistent","ephemeral"] |  |
| permissions.defaultMode | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PermissionMode.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| permissions.providerModes | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PermissionMode.json"}} |
| approvals.policy | yes | ["ask","on-failure","never"] |  |
| threads.useWorktree | yes | boolean |  |
| threads.autoSettleAfter | yes | ["1d","2d","1w","never"] |  |
| threads.unresponsiveAfter | yes | ["2m","5m","15m"] |  |
| threads.settleOnMerge | yes | boolean |  |
| threads.settleOnClose | yes | boolean |  |
| remote.enabled | yes | boolean |  |
| remote.transport | yes | ["local","lan","tailscale","relay"] |  |
| remote.relayUrl | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Empty, or a credential-free WSS relay URL without query or fragment. WS is allowed only on 127.0.0.1.","examples":["","wss://relay.example.invalid/"]} |
| automations.enabled | yes | boolean |  |
| clients.theme | yes | [schema7](schema/schema7) |  |
| clients.keybindings | yes | [schema8](schema/schema8) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "approvals.policy": "on-failure",
  "automations.enabled": true,
  "browser.allowedOrigins": [],
  "browser.backend": "headless",
  "browser.backendLoss": "headless",
  "browser.profile": "ephemeral",
  "clients.keybindings": 5,
  "clients.theme": {},
  "host.displayName": "example",
  "host.icon": {
    "kind": "phone"
  },
  "permissions.defaultMode": null,
  "permissions.providerModes": {},
  "projects.roots": [],
  "providers.coder.model": "example",
  "providers.coder.provider": "codex",
  "providers.coder.reasoningEffort": "max",
  "providers.coder.tier": "standard",
  "providers.configuration": [],
  "providers.default": "acp",
  "providers.planner.model": "example",
  "providers.planner.provider": "antigravity",
  "providers.planner.reasoningEffort": "xhigh",
  "providers.planner.tier": "standard",
  "providers.reviewer.model": "example",
  "providers.reviewer.provider": "claude",
  "providers.reviewer.reasoningEffort": "low",
  "providers.reviewer.tier": "fast",
  "remote.enabled": false,
  "remote.relayUrl": "",
  "remote.transport": "lan",
  "sourceControl.preferences": {
    "host": "github.com",
    "hosts": [
      {
        "host": "github.com"
      }
    ],
    "remote": "origin"
  },
  "threads.autoSettleAfter": "2d",
  "threads.continueAfterRestart": false,
  "threads.followUpBehavior": "steer",
  "threads.limitPolicy": "resume_at_reset",
  "threads.settleOnClose": false,
  "threads.settleOnMerge": true,
  "threads.unresponsiveAfter": "5m",
  "threads.useWorktree": false
}
```

## SourceControlPreferences

[JSON Schema](schema/SourceControlPreferences.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Hosts are unique and the selected host must be configured

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hosts | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"host":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubHost.json"},"account":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubAccount.json"}},"required":["host"]}} |
| host | yes | [GitHubHost.json](schema/GitHubHost.json) |  |
| remote | yes | string | {"maxLength":100,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._-]*$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "host": "github.com",
  "hosts": [
    {
      "host": "github.com"
    }
  ],
  "remote": "origin"
}
```

| kind | yes | [ToolKind.json](schema/ToolKind.json) |  |
| title | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [ToolStatus.json](schema/ToolStatus.json) |  |
| detail | yes | [ToolDetail.json](schema/ToolDetail.json) |  |
| backgroundTaskId | no | [BackgroundTaskId.json](schema/BackgroundTaskId.json) |  |
| error | no | string |  |
| result | no | [ToolResult.json](schema/ToolResult.json) |  |
| startedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| raw | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "agentId": "example",

    "kind": "agent.message",
    "targetAgentId": "example"

  "endedAt": 6,
  "id": "example",
  "kind": "file.read",
  "raw": [],
  "startedAt": 8,
  "status": "failed",
  "title": "example"
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

| type | yes | `"usage.updated"` |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| inputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cachedInputTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json
{
  "change": {
    "id": "example",
    "op": "example",
    "path": "example",
    "version": "example"
  },
  "type": "workspace.files_changed",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## WorktreeCreationEvent

[JSON Schema](schema/WorktreeCreationEvent.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| commandId | yes | [CommandId.json](schema/CommandId.json) |  |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| attempt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| state | yes | ["running","cancelling","cancelled","failed","done","local"] |  |
| step | yes | ["preparing","fetching","creating","checking_out","setup","done"] |  |
| percent | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| startedAt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| elapsedMs | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| steps | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"step":{"type":"string","enum":["preparing","fetching","creating","checking_out","setup","done"]},"startedAt":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"elapsedMs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["step","startedAt","elapsedMs"]}} |
| details | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}} |
| message | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| cleanupComplete | yes | boolean |  |
| actions | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["cancel","local","retry"]}} |
| type | yes | `"worktree.creation.progress"` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "actions": [],
  "attempt": 0,
  "cleanupComplete": false,
  "commandId": "example",
  "details": [],
  "elapsedMs": 5,
  "percent": 3,
  "startedAt": 0,
  "state": "local",
  "step": "preparing",
  "steps": [],
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "worktree.creation.progress"
}
```
